Eight independently checked local interfaces

All eight preselected local-interface requests were accepted by the unchanged native checker. Every whole computation passed independent replay, matching the exact parser input/output and all microstep, physical-step and FNV fingerprints.

No commutativity theorem proof has been searched for or certified. Eight fresh interfaces are now admitted; one byte-identical prior interface admission is reusable; the remaining 85 fresh interfaces are unadmitted. A complete admission batch and target search have not started.

The intended target remains \(\forall\,\mathit{x}\;\left(\forall\,\mathit{y}\;\left(\left(\mathit{x}+\mathit{y}\right)=\left(\mathit{y}+\mathit{x}\right)\right)\right)\). Authored generic local inference infrastructure is distinct from the two automatically discovered theorem certificates in the reusable inventory. This pilot checks that infrastructure under its exact premises. It is not a GCTS proof discovery, cluster search comparison, RL training result or evidence of outperforming an existing prover.

Eight generic size-stratum winners, frozen before all outcomes

The complete 94-request inventory was partitioned by operation and equation origin. Before capacity or native outcomes, the largest canonical whole-request JSON byte count in every nonempty stratum was selected, with exact definition name breaking ties. This native pilot uses exactly those eight frozen winners and their original order. Both complete induction alternatives remain declared; the pilot selects an interface solely by the predeclared size rule. No induction variable or target proof path was chosen by hand.

Measured checking costs and a separate next-stage estimate

ComponentSecondsPeak RSS bytesNative microsteps
Eight native admissions including compile/setup223.789274,661,37630,127,124,355
Independent full native replay including compile/setup223.965271,613,95230,127,124,355

Production and independent replay each used a separately frozen 900-second budget checked before each query, a 512 MiB host-process ceiling and the same 20-billion native-microstep limit per request. Neither had a one-query budget overshoot or native unknown/rejection. All eight requests were fresh complete requests: no earlier native acceptance was reused in these measurements. The complete grammar, previous capacity pilot, original full memory unknown, partial replay and streaming repair remain in their immutable resource ledgers. Manual infrastructure development is unmeasured.

Using each measured size-stratum winner to scale the complete tree-instruction count of its remaining same-stratum requests gives a nominal 706.295 seconds for 85 fresh native admissions plus 707.560 seconds for independent replay. This is one deterministic calibration per stratum, not a bound, confidence interval or completed admission bill. Unmeasured local requests can have different heap/comparison behavior. The earlier two-certificate extrapolation remains preserved in the capacity report.

The recommendation is to freeze the remaining 85 exact requests in two deterministic ascending-name chunks of 43 and 42, keep the current workspace and per-query checker limit, and give each chunk a 900-second production gate with full independent replay before inventory admission. Every unknown remains explicit. This is a proposed next phase requiring parent authorization; no such batch ran. Only after the full registry passes should equal-inventory complete-graph GCTS and conforming cluster-proposal controls run. Ordinary forward proof extraction should remain a separate matched control.
